H87: WHERE THE UNACCOUNTED CYCLES GO.
Apple M4. Run 2026-11-22 22:07 IST. Thresholds fixed before this data
existed, in the commit named below, which also introduced the stallprobe
mode. H86 located 7.77 percent of the acquire-release arm's contended cost
as cycles in which the scheduler issued at least one uop and 0.16 percent
as cycles in which nothing issued because of the backend, leaving 201.186
cycles per message, 92.07 percent, unlocated. Counters FIXED_CYCLES,
ARM_STALL, ARM_STALL_FRONTEND and ARM_STALL_BACKEND, each read from its
description in results/kperf-events.txt. The SLOT variants are not used
because they count slots and a cycle partition is what this needs.
See the H87 row in docs/experiments.md.
